    64→    "body": "_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟡 Minor_\n\n**Failure in the post-creation list refresh (line 96) overwrites the creation success with a generic error.**\n\nIf `createInviteLink` succeeds but the subsequent `fetchInvitesForItem` call on line 96 throws, the catch block on line 98 will display \"failed to create invite link\" even though the invite was created and URL was copied. Consider separating these error paths.\n\n\n<details>\n<summary>Proposed fix</summary>\n\n```diff\n       setSuccess(`link copied to clipboard: ${displayUrl}`);\n \n       // Refresh invite list\n-      const updatedInvites = await fetchInvitesForItem(ipnsName);\n-      setInvites(updatedInvites);\n+      try {\n+        const updatedInvites = await fetchInvitesForItem(ipnsName);\n+        setInvites(updatedInvites);\n+      } catch {\n+        // List refresh failed, but invite was created successfully\n+      }\n     } catch (err) {\n       console.error('Failed to create invite link:', err);\n```\n</details>\n\n<details>\n<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn `@apps/web/src/components/file-browser/InviteLinkTab.tsx` around lines 70 -\n105, The handleCreate function currently wraps both createInviteLink and\nfetchInvitesForItem in a single try/catch so a failure during\nfetchInvitesForItem overwrites the successful creation message; split the logic\nso createInviteLink (and clipboard write + setSuccess) are awaited in their own\ntry block and only then call fetchInvitesForItem inside a separate try/catch; on\nfetch failure log the error and setError (or ignore) but do not clear or\noverwrite the previously setSuccess; refer to handleCreate, createInviteLink,\nfetchInvitesForItem, setSuccess, setError, and setInvites when making this\nchange.\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- fingerprinting:phantom:medusa:phoenix -->\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit -->"

    72→    "body": "_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟡 Minor_\n\n**Success message claims clipboard copy even when it may have silently failed.**\n\nLines 84-89 swallow the clipboard error, but line 93 unconditionally shows \"link copied to clipboard\". If clipboard access is denied (insecure context, permissions), the user sees a misleading message and has no way to retrieve the URL since the ephemeral key is not stored.\n\n\n<details>\n<summary>Proposed fix</summary>\n\n```diff\n       // Auto-copy URL to clipboard\n+      let copied = false;\n       try {\n         await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);\n+        copied = true;\n       } catch {\n         // Clipboard may not be available (e.g., insecure context)\n-        // URL is still created successfully\n       }\n \n       // Truncate URL for display\n       const displayUrl = url.length > 60 ? `${url.slice(0, 30)}...${url.slice(-20)}` : url;\n-      setSuccess(`link copied to clipboard: ${displayUrl}`);\n+      setSuccess(copied ? `link copied to clipboard: ${displayUrl}` : `link created (copy failed -- save this): ${displayUrl}`);\n```\n</details>\n\n<details>\n<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn `@apps/web/src/components/file-browser/InviteLinkTab.tsx` around lines 83 - 93,\nThe current code swallows navigator.clipboard.writeText errors but always calls\nsetSuccess(\"link copied to clipboard...\"), which is misleading and can lose the\nephemeral URL; update InviteLinkTab so the clipboard write result controls the\nmessage: attempt navigator.clipboard.writeText(url) and on success call\nsetSuccess with the truncated displayUrl, but on failure setSuccess to a\ndifferent message that includes the truncated displayUrl (or a clear \"copy\nfailed — here is the link\" text) and ensure the raw url is stored in component\nstate (e.g., inviteUrl/url state) or rendered so the user can manually copy it;\nreference navigator.clipboard.writeText, displayUrl, setSuccess and the url\nvariable when making the change.\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- fingerprinting:phantom:medusa:phoenix -->\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit -->"

    80→    "body": "_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_\n\n**Folder key re-wrapping (Line 74) is not wrapped in try-catch, unlike file keys (Line 53).**\n\nIf `reWrapEncryptedKey` throws for a subfolder's `folderKeyEncrypted`, it propagates up and aborts processing of all remaining siblings.     88→    "body": "_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟡 Minor_\n\n**Ephemeral key lingers in the URL after being read — strip it on mount.**\n\nIn `InvitePage.tsx` (the relevant snippet, lines 60–63), `ephemeralKeyRef` is correctly initialised from `searchParams.get('key')` and zeroed after the claim. However the URL itself is never cleaned up: the key stays visible in the address bar and is written to browser history. Because this is a HashRouter app everything after `#` is client-only, so the server never sees it — but the key can still be extracted from browser history before the invite is claimed.\n\nAdd a `navigate` call immediately after reading the key from the URL:\n\n<details>\n<summary>🛡️ Proposed fix in <code>InvitePage.tsx</code> — strip the key from the URL on mount</summary>\n\n```diff\n // Store ephemeral key in ref -- not state (avoid re-render loss), never log\n const ephemeralKeyRef = useRef<string | null>(searchParams.get('key'));\n+\n+// Strip the key from the address bar / history immediately after reading it\n+useEffect(() => {\n+  if (token && ephemeralKeyRef.current) {\n+    navigate(`/invite/${token}`, { replace: true });\n+  }\n+}, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n```\n</details>\n\n<details>\n<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn `@apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx` around lines 6 - 13, InvitePage reads the\nephemeral key into ephemeralKeyRef from searchParams.get('key') but never\nremoves it from the URL; update the mount logic in InvitePage (where\nephemeralKeyRef is set) to immediately call the router navigate to the same path\nwithout the key query param (use the existing navigate from react-router and\npass replace: true) so the key is stripped from the address bar and not written\nas a new history entry; ensure you construct the new location from the current\npathname and any other safe params but omit 'key'.\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:churro -->\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit -->"

    96→    "body": "_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🔴 Critical_\n\n**Critical: `useSearchParams` reads query params, not the URL hash fragment.**\n\nThe planning docs specify the URL format as `/invite/:token#ephemeralPrivateKey` with the ephemeral key in the hash fragment (never sent to server for zero-knowledge). However, `useSearchParams` reads from `?key=...` query string, not from `#...` hash fragment. This means either:\n\n1. The key is placed in query params (`?key=...`), which **sends the key to the server** in request URLs, violating the zero-knowledge design.\n2. The key is placed in the hash fragment as designed, but `useSearchParams().get('key')` returns `null` and every invite link shows \"invalid link\".\n\nYou need to read from `window.location.hash` instead.\n\n\n\n<details>\n<summary>🔒 Proposed fix: read ephemeral key from hash fragment</summary>\n\n```diff\n- const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  // ...\n- const ephemeralKeyRef = useRef<string | null>(searchParams.get('key'));\n+ const ephemeralKeyRef = useRef<string | null>(() => {\n+   // Read ephemeral private key from hash fragment (never sent to server)\n+   const hash = window.location.hash.slice(1); // remove '#'\n+   return hash || null;\n+ });\n```\n\nAlternatively, if the hash uses `key=value` format (e.g., `#key=abc123`):\n\n```diff\n- const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();\n+ // Parse key from URL hash fragment (zero-knowledge: never sent to server)\n+ const hashParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.hash.slice(1));\n  // ...\n- const ephemeralKeyRef = useRef<string | null>(searchParams.get('key'));\n+ const ephemeralKeyRef = useRef<string | null>(hashParams.get('key'));\n```\n\nThen remove the `useSearchParams` import if unused.\n</details>\n\nAs per coding guidelines: `**/*.{ts,tsx}`: \"Never log, console.log, or persist to disk sensitive data including ECDSA private keys, decrypted file content, plaintext passwords, or key material.\" — placing the key in query params would expose it in server logs.\n\n<details>\n<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn `@apps/web/src/routes/InvitePage.tsx` around lines 44 - 58, useSearchParams is\nreading query params but the ephemeral key is in the URL hash fragment, so\nchange how ephemeralKeyRef is initialized: read window.location.hash (or parse\nkey=value from the hash like \"#key=...\") and set ephemeralKeyRef.current to that\nvalue instead of using useSearchParams.get('key'); update any code that depends\non ephemeralKeyRef accordingly and remove the unused useSearchParams import;\nensure you do not log or persist the key anywhere (no console.log or storage).\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- fingerprinting:phantom:medusa:phoenix -->\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit -->"

     2→  Controller,
     3→  Get,
     4→  Post,

     6→  Param,

    10→} from '@nestjs/common';
    11→import { ApiTags, ApiOperation, ApiResponse, ApiBearerAuth, ApiParam } from '@nestjs/swagger';
    12→import { ThrottlerGuard } from '@nestjs/throttler';
    13→import { JwtAuthGuard } from '../auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard';
    14→import { SharesService } from './shares.service';
    15→import { ClaimInviteDto } from './dto/claim-invite.dto';
    16→import { InviteStatusResponseDto, InviteDataResponseDto } from './dto/invite-response.dto';
    17→
    18→interface RequestWithUser extends Request {
    19→  user: {
    20→    id: string;
    21→  };
    22→}
    23→
    24→/**
    25→ * Public-facing invite controller at /invites prefix.
    26→ * NO class-level auth guard -- individual endpoints opt in.
    27→ */
    28→@ApiTags('invites')
    29→@Controller('invites')
    30→export class InvitesController {
    31→  constructor(private readonly sharesService: SharesService) {}
    32→
    33→  /**
    34→   * PUBLIC: No auth required -- returns only the invite status.
    35→   * Used by the invite landing page before the recipient logs in.
    36→   * Opaque: no file name, no sharer identity.
    37→   */
    38→  @Get(':token')
    39→  @UseGuards(ThrottlerGuard)
    40→  @ApiOperation({
    41→    summary: 'Get invite status (public)',
    42→    description:
    43→      'Check the status of an invite link. Returns only status -- ' +
    44→      'no file name or sharer identity is revealed before authentication.',
    45→  })
    46→  @ApiParam({ name: 'token', description: 'Invite token (URL-safe base64)' })
    47→  @ApiResponse({
    48→    status: 200,
    49→    description: 'Invite status',
    50→    type: InviteStatusResponseDto,
    51→  })
    52→  async getInviteStatus(@Param('token') token: string): Promise<{ status: string }> {
    53→    const result = await this.sharesService.getInviteStatus(token);
    54→    return { status: result?.status ?? 'expired' };
    55→  }
    56→
    57→  /**
    58→   * AUTHENTICATED: Returns full invite data needed for the claim flow.
    59→   * The client uses the encrypted key ciphertext to unwrap with the
    60→   * ephemeral private key (from URL fragment) and re-wrap with their own key.
    61→   */
    62→  @Get(':token/data')
    63→  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, ThrottlerGuard)
    64→  @ApiBearerAuth()
    65→  @ApiOperation({
    66→    summary: 'Get invite data for claim flow (authenticated)',
    67→    description:
    68→      'Fetch full invite data including encrypted key ciphertext. ' +
    69→      'Requires authentication. Used by the client claim flow.',
    70→  })
    71→  @ApiParam({ name: 'token', description: 'Invite token (URL-safe base64)' })
    72→  @ApiResponse({
    73→    status: 200,
    74→    description: 'Full invite data for claim flow',
    75→    type: InviteDataResponseDto,
    76→  })
    77→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
    78→  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Invite not found or expired' })
    79→  async getInviteData(@Param('token') token: string): Promise<{
    80→    status: string;
    81→    encryptedKey: string;
    82→    encryptedChildKeys: Array<{
    83→      keyType: 'file' | 'folder';
    84→      itemId: string;
    85→      encryptedKey: string;
    86→    }> | null;
    87→    itemType: string;
    88→    ipnsName: string;
    89→    itemName: string;
    90→  }> {
    91→    const invite = await this.sharesService.getInviteForClaim(token);
    92→
    93→    if (!invite) {
    94→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
    95→    }
    96→
    97→    return {
    98→      status: invite.status,
    99→      encryptedKey: invite.encryptedKey.toString('hex'),
   100→      encryptedChildKeys: invite.encryptedChildKeys,
   101→      itemType: invite.itemType,
   102→      ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   103→      itemName: invite.itemName,
   104→    };
   105→  }
   106→
   107→  /**
   108→   * AUTHENTICATED: Claim an invite by providing re-wrapped keys.
   109→   * Creates Share + ShareKey records via the existing sharing infrastructure.
   110→   */
   111→  @Post(':token/claim')
   112→  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, ThrottlerGuard)
   113→  @ApiBearerAuth()
   114→  @ApiOperation({
   115→    summary: 'Claim an invite link',
   116→    description:
   117→      'Claim an invite by providing the item key re-wrapped for the recipient. ' +
   118→      'Creates Share and ShareKey records. Single-claim enforced atomically.',
   119→  })
   120→  @ApiParam({ name: 'token', description: 'Invite token (URL-safe base64)' })
   121→  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, description: 'Invite claimed successfully' })
   122→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
   123→  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Invite not found or expired' })
   124→  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Invite already claimed or self-claim' })
   125→  async claimInvite(
   126→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
   127→    @Param('token') token: string,
   128→    @Body() dto: ClaimInviteDto
   129→  ): Promise<{ shareId: string }> {
   130→    return this.sharesService.claimInvite(token, req.user.id, dto);
   131→  }
   132→}
   133→

    27→
    28→/**
    29→ * Authenticated invite management controller at /shares/invites prefix.
    30→ * All endpoints require authentication (class-level JwtAuthGuard).
    31→ */
    32→@ApiTags('share-invites')
    33→@ApiBearerAuth()
    34→@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, ThrottlerGuard)
    35→@Controller('shares/invites')
    36→export class ShareInvitesController {
    37→  constructor(private readonly sharesService: SharesService) {}
    38→
    39→  /**
    40→   * Create a new invite link for sharing a file or folder.
    41→   * Returns the invite token for URL construction on the client.
    42→   */
    43→  @Post()
    44→  @ApiOperation({
    45→    summary: 'Create an invite link',
    46→    description:
    47→      'Create a new invite link with the item key wrapped by an ephemeral public key. ' +
    48→      'Returns the invite token for URL construction. Default expiry: 7 days.',
    49→  })
    50→  @ApiResponse({
    51→    status: 201,
    52→    description: 'Invite created',
    53→    type: InviteResponseDto,
    54→  })
    55→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
    56→  async createInvite(
    57→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
    58→    @Body() dto: CreateInviteDto
    59→  ): Promise<{
    60→    id: string;
    61→    token: string;
    62→    itemType: string;
    63→    ipnsName: string;
    64→    itemName: string;
    65→    status: string;
    66→    expiresAt: Date;
    67→    createdAt: Date;
    68→  }> {
    69→    const invite = await this.sharesService.createInvite(req.user.id, dto);
    70→    return {
    71→      id: invite.id,
    72→      token: invite.token,
    73→      itemType: invite.itemType,
    74→      ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
    75→      itemName: invite.itemName,
    76→      status: invite.status,
    77→      expiresAt: invite.expiresAt,
    78→      createdAt: invite.createdAt,
    79→    };
    80→  }
    81→
    82→  /**
    83→   * List active (unclaimed, unexpired) invites for a specific item.
    84→   * Requires ipnsName query parameter.
    85→   */
    86→  @Get()
    87→  @ApiOperation({
    88→    summary: 'List active invites for an item',
    89→    description:
    90→      'Get all active (unclaimed, unexpired) invite links created by the authenticated user ' +
    91→      'for the specified item. Expired invites are auto-cleaned.',
    92→  })
    93→  @ApiQuery({
    94→    name: 'ipnsName',
    95→    description: 'IPNS name of the item to list invites for',
    96→    required: true,
    97→  })
    98→  @ApiResponse({
    99→    status: 200,
   100→    description: 'List of active invites',
   101→    type: [InviteResponseDto],
   102→  })
   103→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
   104→  async listInvites(
   105→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
   106→    @Query('ipnsName') ipnsName: string
   107→  ): Promise<
   108→    Array<{
   109→      id: string;
   110→      token: string;
   111→      itemType: string;
   112→      ipnsName: string;
   113→      itemName: string;
   114→      status: string;
   115→      expiresAt: Date;
   116→      createdAt: Date;
   117→    }>
   118→  > {
   119→    const invites = await this.sharesService.getInvitesForItem(req.user.id, ipnsName);
   120→    return invites.map((inv) => ({
   121→      id: inv.id,
   122→      token: inv.token,
   123→      itemType: inv.itemType,
   124→      ipnsName: inv.ipnsName,
   125→      itemName: inv.itemName,
   126→      status: inv.status,
   127→      expiresAt: inv.expiresAt,
   128→      createdAt: inv.createdAt,
   129→    }));
   130→  }
   131→
   132→  /**
   133→   * Revoke an active invite link.
   134→   * Only the sharer can revoke. Already-claimed shares are unaffected.
   135→   */
   136→  @Delete(':inviteId')
   137→  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
   138→  @ApiOperation({
   139→    summary: 'Revoke an invite link',
   140→    description:
   141→      'Revoke an active invite link. Only the original sharer can revoke. ' +
   142→      'Already-claimed shares persist independently.',
   143→  })
   144→  @ApiResponse({ status: 204, description: 'Invite revoked' })
   145→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
   146→  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: 'Only the sharer can revoke' })
   147→  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Invite not found' })
   148→  async revokeInvite(
   149→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
   150→    @Param('inviteId', ParseUUIDPipe) inviteId: string
   151→  ): Promise<void> {
   152→    await this.sharesService.revokeInvite(inviteId, req.user.id);
   153→  }
   154→}
   155→

     1→import { useState, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import type { FolderChild } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
     3→import { createInviteLink, fetchInvitesForItem, revokeInvite } from '../../services/invite.service';
     4→import type { InviteInfo } from '../../services/invite.service';
     5→
     6→type InviteLinkTabProps = {
     7→  item: FolderChild;
     8→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
     9→  ipnsName: string;
    10→  parentFolderId: string;
    11→  isOpen: boolean;
    12→};
    13→
    14→/**
    15→ * Invite Link tab content for the ShareDialog.
    16→ *
    17→ * Allows users to:
    18→ * - Create an invite link (URL auto-copied to clipboard)
    19→ * - View active (unclaimed, unexpired) invite links with metadata
    20→ * - Revoke individual invite links with inline confirm
    21→ *
    22→ * IMPORTANT: The ephemeral private key is NOT stored after creation.
    23→ * The URL is copied to clipboard at creation time only. Existing invites
    24→ * show metadata and revoke action, but NOT a copy action.
    25→ */
    26→export function InviteLinkTab({
    27→  item,
    28→  folderKey,
    29→  ipnsName,
    30→  parentFolderId,
    31→  isOpen,
    32→}: InviteLinkTabProps) {
    33→  const [invites, setInvites] = useState<InviteInfo[]>([]);
    34→  const [invitesLoading, setInvitesLoading] = useState(true);
    35→  const [isCreating, setIsCreating] = useState(false);
    36→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    37→  const [success, setSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
    38→  const [confirmRevokeId, setConfirmRevokeId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    39→  const [revokingId, setRevokingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    40→
    41→  // Fetch active invites when tab becomes visible
    42→  useEffect(() => {
    43→    if (!isOpen) return;
    44→
    45→    let cancelled = false;
    46→    setInvitesLoading(true);
    47→
    48→    fetchInvitesForItem(ipnsName)
    49→      .then((result) => {
    50→        if (!cancelled) {
    51→          setInvites(result);
    52→        }
    53→      })
    54→      .catch((err) => {
    55→        if (!cancelled) {
    56→          console.error('Failed to fetch invites:', err);
    57→        }
    58→      })
    59→      .finally(() => {
    60→        if (!cancelled) {
    61→          setInvitesLoading(false);
    62→        }
    63→      });
    64→
    65→    return () => {
    66→      cancelled = true;
    67→    };
    68→  }, [isOpen, ipnsName]);
    69→
    70→  const handleCreate = useCallback(async () => {
    71→    setError(null);
    72→    setSuccess(null);
    73→    setIsCreating(true);
    74→
    75→    try {
    76→      const { url } = await createInviteLink({
    77→        item,
    78→        folderKey,
    79→        ipnsName,
    80→        parentFolderId,
    81→      });
    82→
    83→      // Auto-copy URL to clipboard
    84→      try {
    85→        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
    86→      } catch {
    87→        // Clipboard may not be available (e.g., insecure context)
    88→        // URL is still created successfully
    89→      }
    90→
    91→      // Truncate URL for display
    92→      const displayUrl = url.length > 60 ? `${url.slice(0, 30)}...${url.slice(-20)}` : url;
    93→      setSuccess(`link copied to clipboard: ${displayUrl}`);
    94→
    95→      // Refresh invite list
    96→      const updatedInvites = await fetchInvitesForItem(ipnsName);
    97→      setInvites(updatedInvites);
    98→    } catch (err) {
    99→      console.error('Failed to create invite link:', err);
   100→      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'failed to create invite link';
   101→      setError(message);
   102→    } finally {
   103→      setIsCreating(false);
   104→    }
   105→  }, [item, folderKey, ipnsName, parentFolderId]);
   106→
   107→  const handleRevoke = useCallback(async (inviteId: string) => {
   108→    setRevokingId(inviteId);
   109→    setConfirmRevokeId(null);
   110→    setError(null);
   111→
   112→    try {
   113→      await revokeInvite(inviteId);
   114→      setInvites((prev) => prev.filter((inv) => inv.id !== inviteId));
   115→    } catch (err) {
   116→      console.error('Failed to revoke invite:', err);
   117→      setError('revoke failed');
   118→    } finally {
   119→      setRevokingId(null);
   120→    }
   121→  }, []);
   122→
   123→  /**
   124→   * Format a date string for display: "Feb 23, 2026"
   125→   */
   126→  const formatDate = (dateStr: string): string => {
   127→    try {
   128→      const d = new Date(dateStr);
   129→      return d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
   130→        month: 'short',
   131→        day: 'numeric',
   132→        year: 'numeric',
   133→      });
   134→    } catch {
   135→      return dateStr;
   136→    }
   137→  };
   138→
   139→  return (
   140→    <div className="invite-link-tab" role="tabpanel">
   141→      {/* Create invite link button */}
   142→      <div className="invite-link-create">
   143→        <button
   144→          type="button"
   145→          className="invite-link-create-btn"
   146→          onClick={handleCreate}
   147→          disabled={isCreating}
   148→        >
   149→          {isCreating ? 'creating...' : '--create invite link'}
   150→        </button>
   151→      </div>
   152→
   153→      {/* Error message */}
   154→      {error && (
   155→        <div className="share-error" role="alert">
   156→          {'> '}
   157→          {error}
   158→        </div>
   159→      )}
   160→
   161→      {/* Success message */}
   162→      {success && (
   163→        <div className="share-success" role="status">
   164→          {'> '}
   165→          {success}
   166→        </div>
   167→      )}
   168→
   169→      {/* Active invites list */}
   170→      <div className="invite-link-list-section">
   171→        <div className="share-recipients-header">{'// active invite links'}</div>
   172→
   173→        {invitesLoading ? (
   174→          <div className="share-recipients-loading">loading...</div>
   175→        ) : invites.length === 0 ? (
   176→          <div className="share-recipients-empty">no active invite links</div>
   177→        ) : (
   178→          <div className="invite-link-list">
   179→            {invites.map((invite) => (
   180→              <div key={invite.id} className="invite-link-item">
   181→                <div className="invite-link-info">
   182→                  <span className="invite-status-badge">ACTIVE</span>
   183→                  <span className="invite-link-meta">
   184→                    {'created '}
   185→                    {formatDate(invite.createdAt)}
   186→                    {' | expires '}
   187→                    {formatDate(invite.expiresAt)}
   188→                  </span>
   189→                </div>
   190→
   191→                <div className="invite-link-actions">
   192→                  {confirmRevokeId === invite.id ? (
   193→                    <div className="share-revoke-confirm">
   194→                      <span className="share-revoke-confirm-text">{'confirm?'}</span>
   195→                      <button
   196→                        type="button"
   197→                        className="share-revoke-confirm-btn share-revoke-confirm-btn--yes"
   198→                        onClick={() => handleRevoke(invite.id)}
   199→                        disabled={revokingId === invite.id}
   200→                        aria-label="Confirm revoke invite"
   201→                      >
   202→                        {revokingId === invite.id ? '...' : '[y]'}
   203→                      </button>
   204→                      <button
   205→                        type="button"
   206→                        className="share-revoke-confirm-btn share-revoke-confirm-btn--no"
   207→                        onClick={() => setConfirmRevokeId(null)}
   208→                        aria-label="Cancel revoke invite"
   209→                      >
   210→                        [n]
   211→                      </button>
   212→                    </div>
   213→                  ) : (
   214→                    <button
   215→                      type="button"
   216→                      className="share-revoke-btn"
   217→                      onClick={() => setConfirmRevokeId(invite.id)}
   218→                      disabled={revokingId !== null}
   219→                      aria-label="Revoke invite link"
   220→                    >
   221→                      --revoke
   222→                    </button>
   223→                  )}
   224→                </div>
   225→              </div>
   226→            ))}
   227→          </div>
   228→        )}
   229→      </div>
   230→    </div>
   231→  );
   232→}
   233→

     1→/**
     2→ * Shared key-wrapping utilities for ECIES re-wrapping operations.
     3→ *
     4→ * Extracted from ShareDialog to be shared between direct sharing (Phase 14)
     5→ * and invite link sharing (Phase 15). Both flows need to traverse folder
     6→ * metadata and re-wrap descendant keys for a target public key.
     7→ *
     8→ * Security: All plaintext key material is zeroed after use via .fill(0)
     9→ * in finally blocks.
    10→ */
    11→
    12→import type { FolderChild, FolderEntry, FilePointer } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    13→import {
    14→  wrapKey,
    15→  unwrapKey,
    16→  hexToBytes,
    17→  bytesToHex,
    18→  decryptFolderMetadata,
    19→} from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    20→import { resolveFileMetadata } from '../../services/file-metadata.service';
    21→import { resolveIpnsRecord } from '../../services/ipns.service';
    22→import { fetchFromIpfs } from '../api/ipfs';
    23→import type { ChildKeyDto } from '../../api/models/childKeyDto';
    24→
    25→/**
    26→ * Collect all descendant keys from a folder for sharing.
    27→ * Traverses subfolders depth-first, re-wrapping each file and subfolder key
    28→ * for the target public key.
    29→ *
    30→ * Works for both direct share (recipientPubKey) and invite link (ephemeralPubKey)
    31→ * since the target key is parameterized.
    32→ *
    33→ * @param children - Children of the current folder
    34→ * @param folderKey - Decrypted AES key of the current folder (for resolving file metadata)
    35→ * @param ownerPrivateKey - Owner's secp256k1 private key for unwrapping ECIES keys
    36→ * @param targetPubKeyBytes - Target uncompressed secp256k1 public key (recipient or ephemeral)
    37→ * @param onProgress - Callback for progress tracking
    38→ */
    39→export async function collectChildKeys(
    40→  children: FolderChild[],
    41→  folderKey: Uint8Array,
    42→  ownerPrivateKey: Uint8Array,
    43→  targetPubKeyBytes: Uint8Array,
    44→  onProgress: (wrapped: number) => void
    45→): Promise<ChildKeyDto[]> {
    46→  const childKeys: ChildKeyDto[] = [];
    47→  let wrapped = 0;
    48→
    49→  for (const child of children) {
    50→    if (child.type === 'file') {
    51→      const fp = child as FilePointer;
    52→      // Resolve file metadata to get fileKeyEncrypted, then re-wrap for target
    53→      try {
    54→        const { metadata: fileMeta } = await resolveFileMetadata(fp.fileMetaIpnsName, folderKey);
    55→        const reWrappedFileKey = await reWrapEncryptedKey(
    56→          fileMeta.fileKeyEncrypted,
    57→          ownerPrivateKey,
    58→          targetPubKeyBytes
    59→        );
    60→        childKeys.push({
    61→          keyType: 'file' as ChildKeyDto['keyType'],
    62→          itemId: fp.id,
    63→          encryptedKey: reWrappedFileKey,
    64→        });
    65→        wrapped++;
    66→        onProgress(wrapped);
    67→      } catch (err) {
    68→        console.error(`Failed to re-wrap file key for ${fp.name}:`, err);
    69→        // Continue with other children
    70→      }
    71→    } else {
    72→      const folder = child as FolderEntry;
    73→      // Re-wrap the subfolder's folderKey for the target
    74→      const folderKeyRewrapped = await reWrapEncryptedKey(
    75→        folder.folderKeyEncrypted,
    76→        ownerPrivateKey,
    77→        targetPubKeyBytes
    78→      );
    79→      childKeys.push({
    80→        keyType: 'folder' as ChildKeyDto['keyType'],
    81→        itemId: folder.id,
    82→        encryptedKey: folderKeyRewrapped,
    83→      });
    84→      wrapped++;
    85→      onProgress(wrapped);
    86→
    87→      // Recurse into subfolder: resolve its metadata and collect its children
    88→      try {
    89→        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folder.ipnsName);
    90→        if (resolved) {
    91→          const folderKeyBytes = await unwrapKey(
    92→            hexToBytes(folder.folderKeyEncrypted),
    93→            ownerPrivateKey
    94→          );
    95→          try {
    96→            const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
    97→            const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
    98→            const encrypted = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
    99→            const metadata = await decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, folderKeyBytes);
   100→
   101→            const subKeys = await collectChildKeys(
   102→              metadata.children,
   103→              folderKeyBytes,
   104→              ownerPrivateKey,
   105→              targetPubKeyBytes,
   106→              (subWrapped) => {
   107→                onProgress(wrapped + subWrapped);
   108→              }
   109→            );
   110→            wrapped += subKeys.length;
   111→            childKeys.push(...subKeys);
   112→          } finally {
   113→            folderKeyBytes.fill(0);
   114→          }
   115→        }
   116→      } catch (err) {
   117→        console.error(`Failed to traverse subfolder ${folder.name}:`, err);
   118→        // Continue with other children
   119→      }
   120→    }
   121→  }
   122→
   123→  return childKeys;
   124→}
   125→
   126→/**
   127→ * Re-wrap an ECIES-encrypted hex key from owner to target.
   128→ *
   129→ * Unwraps with ownerPrivateKey, then wraps for targetPubKey.
   130→ * Plaintext key is zeroed after use.
   131→ */
   132→export async function reWrapEncryptedKey(
   133→  encryptedKeyHex: string,
   134→  ownerPrivateKey: Uint8Array,
   135→  targetPubKey: Uint8Array
   136→): Promise<string> {
   137→  const plainKey = await unwrapKey(hexToBytes(encryptedKeyHex), ownerPrivateKey);
   138→  try {
   139→    const rewrapped = await wrapKey(plainKey, targetPubKey);
   140→    return bytesToHex(rewrapped);
   141→  } finally {
   142→    plainKey.fill(0);
   143→  }
   144→}
   145→
```

     1→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     2→import { useParams, useSearchParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
     3→import { MatrixBackground } from '../components/MatrixBackground';
     4→import { StagingBanner } from '../components/StagingBanner';
     5→import { GoogleLoginButton } from '../components/auth/GoogleLoginButton';
     6→import { EmailLoginForm } from '../components/auth/EmailLoginForm';
     7→import { WalletLoginButton } from '../components/auth/WalletLoginButton';
     8→import { DeviceWaitingScreen } from '../components/mfa/DeviceWaitingScreen';
     9→import { RecoveryInput } from '../components/mfa/RecoveryInput';
    10→import { checkInviteStatus, claimInvite } from '../services/invite.service';
    11→import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
    12→import '../styles/invite-page.css';
    13→
    14→/** State machine for invite page */
    15→type InvitePageState = 'loading' | 'valid' | 'claiming' | 'claimed' | 'error';
    16→
    17→/** Error reason for display */
    18→type ErrorReason = 'expired' | 'claimed' | 'revoked' | 'invalid';
    19→
    20→type MfaView = 'waiting' | 'recovery';
    21→
    22→const ERROR_MESSAGES: Record<ErrorReason, string> = {
    23→  expired: 'this link has expired',
    24→  claimed: 'this link has already been claimed',
    25→  revoked: 'this link has been revoked',
    26→  invalid: 'invalid link',
    27→};
    28→
    29→/**
    30→ * Invite landing page for share link recipients.
    31→ *
    32→ * Standalone page (no AppShell) with:
    33→ * - Status check on mount via checkInviteStatus
    34→ * - Branded card with login CTA for valid invites
    35→ * - Inline auth (Google, Email, Wallet) matching Login.tsx
    36→ * - Auto-claim after authentication via useEffect watching isAuthenticated
    37→ * - Error cards with red border for expired/claimed/revoked/invalid
    38→ *
    39→ * The ephemeral private key is read from the hash fragment query param
    40→ * on initial render and stored in a ref (never in state, never logged).
    41→ */
    42→export function InvitePage() {
    43→  const { token } = useParams<{ token: string }>();
    44→  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    45→  const navigate = useNavigate();
    46→
    47→  const {
    48→    isAuthenticated,
    49→    isLoading,
    50→    isRequiredShare,
    51→    loginWithGoogle,
    52→    loginWithEmail,
    53→    loginWithWallet,
    54→    completeRequiredShare,
    55→  } = useAuth();
    56→
    57→  // Store ephemeral key in ref -- not state (avoid re-render loss), never log
    58→  const ephemeralKeyRef = useRef<string | null>(searchParams.get('key'));
    59→
    60→  const [pageState, setPageState] = useState<InvitePageState>('loading');
    61→  const [errorReason, setErrorReason] = useState<ErrorReason>('invalid');
    62→  const [loginError, setLoginError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    63→  const [mfaView, setMfaView] = useState<MfaView>('waiting');
    64→
    65→  // Track whether we've started claiming to prevent double-claim
    66→  const claimingRef = useRef(false);
    67→
    68→  // Validate URL params and check invite status on mount
    69→  useEffect(() => {
    70→    if (!token || !ephemeralKeyRef.current) {
    71→      setErrorReason('invalid');
    72→      setPageState('error');
    73→      return;
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    let cancelled = false;
    77→
    78→    checkInviteStatus(token).then((status) => {
    79→      if (cancelled) return;
    80→
    81→      if (status === 'active') {
    82→        setPageState('valid');
    83→      } else {
    84→        setErrorReason(status as ErrorReason);
    85→        setPageState('error');
    86→      }
    87→    });
    88→
    89→    return () => {
    90→      cancelled = true;
    91→    };
    92→  }, [token]);
    93→
    94→  // Auto-claim when authentication completes
    95→  useEffect(() => {
    96→    if (!isAuthenticated || pageState !== 'valid' || claimingRef.current) return;
    97→    if (!token || !ephemeralKeyRef.current) return;
    98→
    99→    claimingRef.current = true;
   100→    setPageState('claiming');
   101→
   102→    const ephemeralKeyHex = ephemeralKeyRef.current;
   103→
   104→    claimInvite(token, ephemeralKeyHex)
   105→      .then(() => {
   106→        setPageState('claimed');
   107→        // Zero the ephemeral key
   108→        ephemeralKeyRef.current = null;
   109→        // Navigate to shared after brief delay
   110→        setTimeout(() => {
   111→          navigate('/shared', { replace: true });
   112→        }, 500);
   113→      })
   114→      .catch((err) => {
   115→        const status = (err as { status?: number }).status;
   116→        if (status === 409) {
   117→          setErrorReason('claimed');
   118→        } else if (status === 404) {
   119→          setErrorReason('expired');
   120→        } else {
   121→          setErrorReason('invalid');
   122→        }
   123→        setPageState('error');
   124→      })
   125→      .finally(() => {
   126→        // Always zero the ephemeral key
   127→        ephemeralKeyRef.current = null;
   128→      });
   129→  }, [isAuthenticated, pageState, token, navigate]);
   130→
   131→  // Auth callbacks -- match Login.tsx patterns
   132→  const handleGoogleLogin = useCallback(
   133→    async (googleIdToken: string) => {
   134→      setLoginError(null);
   135→      try {
   136→        await loginWithGoogle(googleIdToken);
   137→      } catch (err) {
   138→        setLoginError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Google login failed');
   139→      }
   140→    },
   141→    [loginWithGoogle]
   142→  );
   143→
   144→  const handleEmailLogin = useCallback(
   145→    async (email: string, otp: string) => {
   146→      setLoginError(null);
   147→      try {
   148→        await loginWithEmail(email, otp);
   149→      } catch (err) {
   150→        setLoginError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Email login failed');
   151→      }
   152→    },
   153→    [loginWithEmail]
   154→  );
   155→
   156→  const handleWalletLogin = useCallback(
   157→    async (cipherboxJwt: string, userId: string) => {
   158→      setLoginError(null);
   159→      try {
   160→        await loginWithWallet(cipherboxJwt, userId);
   161→      } catch (err) {
   162→        setLoginError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Wallet login failed');
   163→      }
   164→    },
   165→    [loginWithWallet]
   166→  );
   167→
   168→  // MFA handlers -- match Login.tsx patterns
   169→  const handleRecoveryFallback = useCallback(() => {
   170→    setMfaView('recovery');
   171→  }, []);
   172→
   173→  const handleBackToWaiting = useCallback(() => {
   174→    setMfaView('waiting');
   175→  }, []);
   176→
   177→  const handleRecoveryComplete = useCallback(async () => {
   178→    try {
   179→      await completeRequiredShare();
   180→    } catch (err) {
   181→      setLoginError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to complete login after recovery');
   182→    }
   183→  }, [completeRequiredShare]);
   184→
   185→  const handleApprovalComplete = useCallback(() => {
   186→    // Approval flow already called completeRequiredShare() inside the hook.
   187→  }, []);
   188→
   189→  // Shared card wrapper
   190→  const isError = pageState === 'error';
   191→
   192→  // Show loading state while auth is initializing
   193→  if (isLoading && pageState === 'loading') {
   194→    return (
   195→      <>
   196→        <StagingBanner variant="login" />
   197→        <div className="invite-page">
   198→          <MatrixBackground opacity={0.3} frameInterval={50} />
   199→          <div className="invite-card">
   200→            <h1 className="invite-card__title">CIPHERBOX</h1>
   201→            <p className="invite-card__tagline">zero-knowledge encrypted storage</p>
   202→            <div className="invite-card__loading">initializing...</div>
   203→          </div>
   204→        </div>
   205→      </>
   206→    );
   207→  }
   208→
   209→  // REQUIRED_SHARE state: show MFA challenge UI
   210→  if (isRequiredShare && pageState === 'valid') {
   211→    return (
   212→      <>
   213→        <StagingBanner variant="login" />
   214→        <div className="invite-page">
   215→          <MatrixBackground opacity={0.3} frameInterval={50} />
   216→          <div className="invite-card">
   217→            <h1 className="invite-card__title">CIPHERBOX</h1>
   218→            <p className="invite-card__tagline">zero-knowledge encrypted storage</p>
   219→            {mfaView === 'waiting' ? (
   220→              <DeviceWaitingScreen
   221→                onRecoveryFallback={handleRecoveryFallback}
   222→                onApprovalComplete={handleApprovalComplete}
   223→              />
   224→            ) : (
   225→              <RecoveryInput
   226→                onRecoveryComplete={handleRecoveryComplete}
   227→                onBack={handleBackToWaiting}
   228→              />
   229→            )}
   230→            {loginError && (
   231→              <div className="invite-card__login-error" role="alert" aria-live="polite">
   232→                {loginError}
   233→              </div>
   234→            )}
   235→          </div>
   236→        </div>
   237→      </>
   238→    );
   239→  }
   240→
   241→  return (
   242→    <>
   243→      <StagingBanner variant="login" />
   244→      <div className="invite-page">
   245→        <MatrixBackground opacity={0.3} frameInterval={50} />
   246→        <div className={`invite-card${isError ? ' invite-card--error' : ''}`}>
   247→          <h1 className="invite-card__title">CIPHERBOX</h1>
   248→          <p className="invite-card__tagline">zero-knowledge encrypted storage</p>
   249→
   250→          {/* Loading state */}
   251→          {pageState === 'loading' && (
   252→            <div className="invite-card__loading">checking invite...</div>
   253→          )}
   254→
   255→          {/* Valid invite -- show branded message + auth */}
   256→          {pageState === 'valid' && !isAuthenticated && (
   257→            <>
   258→              <p className="invite-card__message">someone shared a file with you</p>
   259→              <p className="invite-card__sub-message">
   260→                log in or create a CipherBox account to access this shared file
   261→              </p>
   262→              <div className="invite-card__auth-area">
   263→                <div className="invite-card__auth-methods">
   264→                  <GoogleLoginButton onLogin={handleGoogleLogin} disabled={isLoading} />
   265→                  <div className="invite-card__auth-divider">
   266→                    <span>{'// or'}</span>
   267→                  </div>
   268→                  <EmailLoginForm onLogin={handleEmailLogin} disabled={isLoading} />
   269→                  <div className="invite-card__auth-divider">
   270→                    <span>{'// or'}</span>
   271→                  </div>
   272→                  <WalletLoginButton onLogin={handleWalletLogin} disabled={isLoading} />
   273→                </div>
   274→              </div>
   275→              {loginError && (
   276→                <div className="invite-card__login-error" role="alert" aria-live="polite">
   277→                  {loginError}
   278→                </div>
   279→              )}
   280→            </>
   281→          )}
   282→
   283→          {/* Claiming state */}
   284→          {pageState === 'claiming' && (
   285→            <div className="invite-card__claiming">claiming your share...</div>
   286→          )}
   287→
   288→          {/* Claimed state -- brief success before redirect */}
   289→          {pageState === 'claimed' && (

   291→          )}
   292→
   293→          {/* Error state */}
   294→          {pageState === 'error' && (

   296→              <div className="invite-card__error">{ERROR_MESSAGES[errorReason]}</div>
   297→              <button type="button" className="invite-card__home-btn" onClick={() => navigate('/')}>

   299→              </button>
   300→            </>
   301→          )}
   302→        </div>
   303→      </div>
   304→    </>
   305→  );
   306→}
   307→

     1→import { HashRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
     2→import { Login } from './Login';
     3→import { FilesPage } from './FilesPage';
     4→import { SharedPage } from './SharedPage';

     7→
     8→export function AppRoutes() {
     9→  return (
    10→    <HashRouter>
    11→      <Routes>

    17→        <Route path="/dashboard" element={<Navigate to="/files" replace />} />
    18→      </Routes>
    19→    </HashRouter>
    20→  );

```
     1→/**
     2→ * Invite Service - Frontend invite link creation and claim with ephemeral key bridge
     3→ *
     4→ * Core security pattern: The sharer generates an ephemeral secp256k1 keypair,
     5→ * wraps the item key with the ephemeral public key, and puts the ephemeral
     6→ * PRIVATE key in the URL fragment (never sent to server). The recipient
     7→ * unwraps with the ephemeral private key and re-wraps with their own public key.
     8→ *
     9→ * All ephemeral and plaintext key material is zeroed in finally blocks.
    10→ */
    11→
    12→import * as secp256k1 from '@noble/secp256k1';
    13→import { wrapKey, unwrapKey, hexToBytes, bytesToHex } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    14→import type { FolderChild, FolderEntry, FilePointer } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    15→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
    16→import {
    17→  invitesControllerGetInviteStatus,
    18→  invitesControllerGetInviteData,
    19→  invitesControllerClaimInvite,
    20→} from '../api/invites/invites';
    21→import {
    22→  shareInvitesControllerCreateInvite,
    23→  shareInvitesControllerListInvites,
    24→  shareInvitesControllerRevokeInvite,
    25→} from '../api/share-invites/share-invites';
    26→import { collectChildKeys } from '../lib/crypto/key-wrapping';
    27→import { resolveFileMetadata } from './file-metadata.service';
    28→import { resolveIpnsRecord } from './ipns.service';
    29→import { fetchFromIpfs } from '../lib/api/ipfs';
    30→import { decryptFolderMetadata } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    31→
    32→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→// Types
    34→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35→
    36→/** Info about an active invite link */
    37→export type InviteInfo = {
    38→  id: string;
    39→  token: string;
    40→  status: string;
    41→  itemType: string;
    42→  ipnsName: string;
    43→  itemName: string;
    44→  expiresAt: string;
    45→  createdAt: string;
    46→};
    47→
    48→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→// Ephemeral keypair generation
    50→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    51→
    52→/**
    53→ * Generate an ephemeral secp256k1 keypair for invite link creation.
    54→ * The private key goes in the URL fragment (never sent to server).
    55→ * The public key wraps the item key (stored on server as ciphertext).
    56→ */
    57→function generateEphemeralKeypair(): {
    58→  privateKey: Uint8Array;
    59→  publicKey: Uint8Array;
    60→  privateKeyHex: string;
    61→} {
    62→  const keypair = secp256k1.keygen();
    63→  return {
    64→    privateKey: keypair.secretKey,
    65→    publicKey: secp256k1.getPublicKey(keypair.secretKey, false), // uncompressed 65-byte key for ECIES
    66→    privateKeyHex: bytesToHex(keypair.secretKey),
    67→  };
    68→}
    69→
    70→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    71→// URL construction
    72→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    73→
    74→/**
    75→ * Build an invite URL with the ephemeral private key in the hash fragment.
    76→ *
    77→ * Format: `${origin}${pathname}#/invite/${token}?key=${ephemeralPrivKeyHex}`
    78→ *
    79→ * The entire path after # is the hash fragment -- never sent to server.
    80→ * React Router's HashRouter parses the route, and useSearchParams()
    81→ * provides access to the key parameter.
    82→ */
    83→export function buildInviteUrl(token: string, ephemeralPrivKeyHex: string): string {
    84→  const base = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname;
    85→  return `${base}#/invite/${token}?key=${ephemeralPrivKeyHex}`;
    86→}
    87→
    88→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89→// Create invite link
    90→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    91→
    92→/**
    93→ * Create an invite link for a file or folder.
    94→ *
    95→ * 1. Generates ephemeral secp256k1 keypair
    96→ * 2. Wraps item key with ephemeral public key
    97→ * 3. Collects and wraps child keys (for folders)
    98→ * 4. Creates invite record on server
    99→ * 5. Builds URL with ephemeral private key in fragment
   100→ * 6. Zeros ephemeral private key from memory
   101→ *
   102→ * @returns The invite URL and token
   103→ */
   104→export async function createInviteLink(params: {
   105→  item: FolderChild;
   106→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
   107→  ipnsName: string;
   108→  parentFolderId: string;
   109→}): Promise<{ url: string; token: string }> {
   110→  const { item, folderKey, ipnsName } = params;
   111→  const ephemeralKeypair = generateEphemeralKeypair();
   112→
   113→  try {
   114→    const vaultKeypair = useAuthStore.getState().vaultKeypair;
   115→    if (!vaultKeypair) {
   116→      throw new Error('Vault keypair not available');
   117→    }
   118→
   119→    const ownerPrivateKey = vaultKeypair.privateKey;
   120→    let encryptedKey: string;
   121→    let encryptedChildKeys:
   122→      | Array<{ keyType: 'file' | 'folder'; itemId: string; encryptedKey: string }>
   123→      | undefined;
   124→
   125→    if (item.type === 'folder') {
   126→      const folderEntry = item as FolderEntry;
   127→
   128→      // Unwrap the folder's own key from its ECIES-encrypted form
   129→      const itemFolderKey = await unwrapKey(
   130→        hexToBytes(folderEntry.folderKeyEncrypted),
   131→        ownerPrivateKey
   132→      );
   133→
   134→      try {
   135→        // Wrap the folder key with ephemeral public key
   136→        const wrappedForEphemeral = await wrapKey(itemFolderKey, ephemeralKeypair.publicKey);
   137→        encryptedKey = bytesToHex(wrappedForEphemeral);
   138→
   139→        // Resolve folder metadata and collect child keys
   140→        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderEntry.ipnsName);
   141→        if (resolved) {
   142→          const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
   143→          const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
   144→          const encrypted = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
   145→          const metadata = await decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, itemFolderKey);
   146→
   147→          encryptedChildKeys = await collectChildKeys(
   148→            metadata.children,
   149→            itemFolderKey,
   150→            ownerPrivateKey,
   151→            ephemeralKeypair.publicKey,
   152→            () => {} // No progress UI for invite creation
   153→          );
   154→        }
   155→      } finally {
   156→        itemFolderKey.fill(0);
   157→      }
   158→    } else {
   159→      // File sharing: wrap parent folder key with ephemeral key
   160→      // (recipient needs parent folder key to decrypt file metadata)
   161→      const filePointer = item as FilePointer;
   162→
   163→      const wrappedFolderKey = await wrapKey(folderKey, ephemeralKeypair.publicKey);
   164→      encryptedKey = bytesToHex(wrappedFolderKey);
   165→
   166→      // Resolve file metadata and wrap the file key as a child key
   167→      const { metadata: fileMeta } = await resolveFileMetadata(
   168→        filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
   169→        folderKey
   170→      );
   171→      const fileKeyPlain = await unwrapKey(hexToBytes(fileMeta.fileKeyEncrypted), ownerPrivateKey);
   172→      try {
   173→        const wrappedFileKey = await wrapKey(fileKeyPlain, ephemeralKeypair.publicKey);
   174→        encryptedChildKeys = [
   175→          {
   176→            keyType: 'file' as const,
   177→            itemId: filePointer.id,
   178→            encryptedKey: bytesToHex(wrappedFileKey),
   179→          },
   180→        ];
   181→      } finally {
   182→        fileKeyPlain.fill(0);
   183→      }
   184→    }
   185→
   186→    // Create invite on server
   187→    const result = await shareInvitesControllerCreateInvite({
   188→      itemType: item.type === 'folder' ? 'folder' : 'file',
   189→      ipnsName,
   190→      itemName: item.name,
   191→      encryptedKey,
   192→      encryptedChildKeys,
   193→    });
   194→
   195→    // Build URL with ephemeral private key in hash fragment
   196→    const url = buildInviteUrl(result.token, ephemeralKeypair.privateKeyHex);
   197→
   198→    return { url, token: result.token };
   199→  } finally {
   200→    // Zero ephemeral private key from memory
   201→    ephemeralKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
   202→  }
   203→}
   204→
   205→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   206→// Claim invite
   207→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   208→
   209→/**
   210→ * Claim an invite link by unwrapping with ephemeral key and re-wrapping with own key.
   211→ *
   212→ * 1. Fetch full invite data from authenticated endpoint
   213→ * 2. Unwrap item key with ephemeral private key
   214→ * 3. Re-wrap with recipient's own public key
   215→ * 4. Repeat for all child keys
   216→ * 5. Zero all sensitive key material
   217→ * 6. POST claim with re-wrapped keys
   218→ *
   219→ * @returns The share ID of the created share
   220→ */
   221→export async function claimInvite(
   222→  token: string,
   223→  ephemeralPrivKeyHex: string
   224→): Promise<{ shareId: string }> {
   225→  const ephemeralPrivKey = hexToBytes(ephemeralPrivKeyHex);
   226→
   227→  try {
   228→    // Fetch full invite data (authenticated endpoint)
   229→    const inviteData = await invitesControllerGetInviteData(token);
   230→
   231→    // Get recipient's own vault keypair
   232→    const vaultKeypair = useAuthStore.getState().vaultKeypair;
   233→    if (!vaultKeypair) {
   234→      throw new Error('Vault keypair not available');
   235→    }
   236→
   237→    // Unwrap item key with ephemeral private key
   238→    const plaintextKey = await unwrapKey(hexToBytes(inviteData.encryptedKey), ephemeralPrivKey);
   239→
   240→    let reWrappedKey: string;
   241→    try {
   242→      // Re-wrap with recipient's own public key
   243→      const wrapped = await wrapKey(plaintextKey, vaultKeypair.publicKey);
   244→      reWrappedKey = bytesToHex(wrapped);
   245→    } finally {
   246→      plaintextKey.fill(0);
   247→    }
   248→
   249→    // Re-wrap child keys
   250→    const childKeys: Array<{
   251→      keyType: 'file' | 'folder';
   252→      itemId: string;
   253→      encryptedKey: string;
   254→    }> = [];
   255→
   256→    const rawChildKeys = inviteData.encryptedChildKeys as Array<{
   257→      keyType: 'file' | 'folder';
   258→      itemId: string;
   259→      encryptedKey: string;
   260→    }> | null;
   261→
   262→    if (rawChildKeys && rawChildKeys.length > 0) {
   263→      for (const ck of rawChildKeys) {
   264→        const plainChildKey = await unwrapKey(hexToBytes(ck.encryptedKey), ephemeralPrivKey);
   265→        try {
   266→          const wrappedChild = await wrapKey(plainChildKey, vaultKeypair.publicKey);
   267→          childKeys.push({
   268→            keyType: ck.keyType,
   269→            itemId: ck.itemId,
   270→            encryptedKey: bytesToHex(wrappedChild),
   271→          });
   272→        } finally {
   273→          plainChildKey.fill(0);
   274→        }
   275→      }
   276→    }
   277→
   278→    // POST claim with re-wrapped keys
   279→    const result = (await invitesControllerClaimInvite(token, {
   280→      encryptedKey: reWrappedKey,
   281→      childKeys,
   282→    })) as unknown as { shareId: string };
   283→
   284→    return { shareId: result.shareId };
   285→  } finally {
   286→    // Zero ephemeral private key
   287→    ephemeralPrivKey.fill(0);
   288→  }
   289→}
   290→
   291→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   292→// Invite status check
   293→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   294→
   295→/**
   296→ * Check the status of an invite link (public, no auth required).
   297→ * Returns the invite status or 'expired' on error/404.
   298→ */
   299→export async function checkInviteStatus(
   300→  token: string
   301→): Promise<'active' | 'expired' | 'claimed' | 'revoked'> {
   302→  try {
   303→    const result = await invitesControllerGetInviteStatus(token);
   304→    return result.status as 'active' | 'expired' | 'claimed' | 'revoked';
   305→  } catch {
   306→    return 'expired';
   307→  }
   308→}
   309→
   310→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   311→// Invite management
   312→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   313→
   314→/**
   315→ * Fetch all active invites for a specific item.
   316→ * Uses the authenticated ShareInvitesController endpoint.
   317→ */
   318→export async function fetchInvitesForItem(ipnsName: string): Promise<InviteInfo[]> {
   319→  const response = await shareInvitesControllerListInvites({ ipnsName });
   320→  return response.map((inv) => ({
   321→    id: inv.id,
   322→    token: inv.token,
   323→    status: inv.status,
   324→    itemType: inv.itemType,
   325→    ipnsName: inv.ipnsName,
   326→    itemName: inv.itemName,
   327→    expiresAt: String(inv.expiresAt),
   328→    createdAt: String(inv.createdAt),
   329→  }));
   330→}
   331→
   332→/**
   333→ * Revoke an active invite link.
   334→ * Only the original sharer can revoke. Already-claimed shares persist.
   335→ */
   336→export async function revokeInvite(inviteId: string): Promise<void> {
   337→  await shareInvitesControllerRevokeInvite(inviteId);
   338→}
   339→
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/InviteLinkTab.tsx",
  "old_string": "      // Auto-copy URL to clipboard\n      try {\n        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);\n      } catch {\n        // Clipboard may not be available (e.g., insecure context)\n        // URL is still created successfully\n      }\n\n      // Truncate URL for display\n      const displayUrl = url.length > 60 ? `${url.slice(0, 30)}...${url.slice(-20)}` : url;\n      setSuccess(`link copied to clipboard: ${displayUrl}`);\n\n      // Refresh invite list\n      const updatedInvites = await fetchInvitesForItem(ipnsName);\n      setInvites(updatedInvites);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error('Failed to create invite link:', err);\n      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'failed to create invite link';\n      setError(message);",
  "new_string": "      // Auto-copy URL to clipboard\n      let copied = false;\n      try {\n        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);\n        copied = true;\n      } catch {\n        // Clipboard may not be available (e.g., insecure context)\n      }\n\n      // Truncate URL for display\n      const displayUrl = url.length > 60 ? `${url.slice(0, 30)}...${url.slice(-20)}` : url;\n      setSuccess(\n        copied\n          ? `link copied to clipboard: ${displayUrl}`\n          : `link created (copy failed -- save this): ${displayUrl}`\n      );\n\n      // Refresh invite list (don't overwrite success on failure)\n      try {\n        const updatedInvites = await fetchInvitesForItem(ipnsName);\n        setInvites(updatedInvites);\n      } catch {\n        // List refresh failed, but invite was created successfully\n      }\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error('Failed to create invite link:', err);\n      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'failed to create invite link';\n      setError(message);"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/lib/crypto/key-wrapping.ts",
  "old_string": "    } else {\n      const folder = child as FolderEntry;\n      // Re-wrap the subfolder's folderKey for the target\n      const folderKeyRewrapped = await reWrapEncryptedKey(\n        folder.folderKeyEncrypted,\n        ownerPrivateKey,\n        targetPubKeyBytes\n      );\n      childKeys.push({\n        keyType: 'folder' as ChildKeyDto['keyType'],\n        itemId: folder.id,\n        encryptedKey: folderKeyRewrapped,\n      });\n      wrapped++;\n      onProgress(wrapped);\n\n      // Recurse into subfolder: resolve its metadata and collect its children\n      try {\n        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folder.ipnsName);\n        if (resolved) {\n          const folderKeyBytes = await unwrapKey(\n            hexToBytes(folder.folderKeyEncrypted),\n            ownerPrivateKey\n          );\n          try {\n            const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);\n            const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);\n            const encrypted = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);\n            const metadata = await decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, folderKeyBytes);\n\n            const subKeys = await collectChildKeys(\n              metadata.children,\n              folderKeyBytes,\n              ownerPrivateKey,\n              targetPubKeyBytes,\n              (subWrapped) => {\n                onProgress(wrapped + subWrapped);\n              }\n            );\n            wrapped += subKeys.length;\n            childKeys.push(...subKeys);\n          } finally {\n            folderKeyBytes.fill(0);\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error(`Failed to traverse subfolder ${folder.name}:`, err);\n        // Continue with other children\n      }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    } else {\n      const folder = child as FolderEntry;\n      // Re-wrap the subfolder's folderKey for the target\n      try {\n        const folderKeyRewrapped = await reWrapEncryptedKey(\n          folder.folderKeyEncrypted,\n          ownerPrivateKey,\n          targetPubKeyBytes\n        );\n        childKeys.push({\n          keyType: 'folder' as ChildKeyDto['keyType'],\n          itemId: folder.id,\n          encryptedKey: folderKeyRewrapped,\n        });\n        wrapped++;\n        onProgress(wrapped);\n\n        // Recurse into subfolder: resolve its metadata and collect its children\n        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folder.ipnsName);\n        if (resolved) {\n          const folderKeyBytes = await unwrapKey(\n            hexToBytes(folder.folderKeyEncrypted),\n            ownerPrivateKey\n          );\n          try {\n            const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);\n            const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);\n            const encrypted = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);\n            const metadata = await decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, folderKeyBytes);\n\n            const subKeys = await collectChildKeys(\n              metadata.children,\n              folderKeyBytes,\n              ownerPrivateKey,\n              targetPubKeyBytes,\n              (subWrapped) => {\n                onProgress(wrapped + subWrapped);\n              }\n            );\n            wrapped += subKeys.length;\n            childKeys.push(...subKeys);\n          } finally {\n            folderKeyBytes.fill(0);\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error(`Failed to re-wrap folder key for ${folder.name}:`, err);\n        // Continue with other children\n      }\n    }"
}

A corrupt subfolder key no longer aborts the entire traversal.\"\n\n**Thread 11** (PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM5wP5yn) - Strip ephemeral key from URL, commentId 2843437504\nReply: \"Valid — added a `useEffect` on mount that calls `navigate(\\`/invite/\\${token}\\`, { replace: true })` to strip the key from the address bar and browser history immediately after reading it into the ref.\"\n\n**Thread 12** (PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM5wP5ys) - useSearchParams vs hash fragment, commentId 2843437510\nReply: \"This is a false positive. The app uses `HashRouter`, so the entire URL after `#` is the client-side route — including the `?key=...` portion. The browser never sends the hash fragment to the server.\\n\\nThe URL format is: `https://app.cipherbox.cc/#/invite/TOKEN?key=EPHEMERAL_KEY`\\n\\nWith `HashRouter`, `useSearchParams()` reads from within the hash fragment, not from the server-visible query string. The key is never included in any HTTP request. This is correct and zero-knowledge safe.\\n\\nSee `buildInviteUrl()` in `invite.service.ts` which constructs the URL as `\\${base}#/invite/\\${token}?key=\\${ephemeralPrivKeyHex}`.\\n\\nThat said, I did add an additional security measure: the key is now stripped from the address bar on mount via `navigate('/invite/TOKEN', { replace: true })` to minimize exposure in browser history.\"\n\n**Thread 13** (PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM5wP5yv) - Missing .catch() on checkInviteStatus, commentId 2843437513\nReply: \"Valid — added `.catch()` handler that sets `errorReason` to `'invalid'` and `pageState` to `'error'`. Respects the `cancelled` flag for unmount safety.\"\n\n**Thread 14** (PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM5wP5y1) - Unsafe cast ErrorReason, commentId 2843437519\nReply: \"Valid — replaced the unsafe cast with explicit validation. Unknown status values now fall back to `'invalid'`.\"\n\n**Thread 15** (PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM5wP5y3) - setTimeout cleanup on unmount, commentId 2843437522\nReply: \"Valid — added `redirectTimerRef` to track the timeout, with a cleanup `useEffect` that clears it on unmount.\"\n\n**Thread 16** (PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM5wP5y5) - invite.service.ts unsafe cast, commentId 2843437524\nReply: \"Fixed — this was a downstream symptom of the missing `ClaimInviteResponseDto` on the backend. Now that the `@ApiResponse` decorator includes the response type, Orval generates the correct return type and the `as unknown as { shareId: string }` cast has been removed.\"\n\n**Thread 17** (PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM5wP5y8) - Design .pen file WCAG contrast, commentId 2843437528\nReply: \"Noted — this is in the design file (`cipher-box-design.pen`), not in application code. The muted green (`#006644`) is an intentional part of the terminal/hacker aesthetic. The actual CSS implementation uses appropriate contrast values. Design file updates are tracked separately from code changes.\"\n\n**Thread 18** (PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM5wP5y9) - Design .pen file copy alignment, commentId 2843437529\nReply: \"The backend TTL is hardcoded to 7 days and single-claim (`max_claims: 1`) — see `shares.service.ts` `createInvite()`. The design copy matches the current implementation. If these become configurable in the future, both the copy and design will be updated together.\"\n\n**Thread 19** (PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM5wP5y_) - openapi.json claim response, commentId 2843437531\nReply: \"Fixed — same root cause as thread 3. Added `ClaimInviteResponseDto` with `type` parameter to the `@ApiResponse` decorator. The regenerated OpenAPI spec now includes the `{ shareId: string }` response schema.\"\n\n**Thread 20** (PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM5wP5zB) - openapi.json encryptedChildKeys, commentId 2843437534\nReply: \"Fixed — same root cause as threads 6/7. Added `type: [InviteChildKeyDto]` to the `@ApiProperty` decorator on `InviteDataResponseDto.encryptedChildKeys`. The regenerated OpenAPI spec now correctly shows `type: array` with `InviteChildKeyDto` items.\"\n\nFor each thread:\n1. Post a reply using: gh api repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/190/comments --field in_reply_to=COMMENT_ID --raw-field body='REPLY_MESSAGE'\n2. Resolve the thread using: gh api graphql -f query='mutation { resolveReviewThread(input: {threadId: \"THREAD_ID\"}) { thread { isResolved } } }'\n\nDo all 20 threads. Use the exact comment IDs and thread IDs provided above. Make sure to escape any special characters in the reply messages for shell safety.\n"
}

The app uses `HashRouter`, so the entire URL after `#` is the client-side route — including the `?key=...` portion. The browser never sends the hash fragment to the server.\n\nThe URL format is: `https://app.cipherbox.cc/#/invite/TOKEN?key=EPHEMERAL_KEY`\n\nWith `HashRouter`, `useSearchParams()` reads from within the hash fragment, not from the server-visible query string. The key is never included in any HTTP request. This is correct and zero-knowledge safe.\n\nSee `buildInviteUrl()` in `invite.service.ts` which constructs the URL as `${base}#/invite/${token}?key=${ephemeralPrivKeyHex}`.\n\nThat said, I did add an additional security measure: the key is now stripped from the address bar on mount via `navigate('\\''/invite/TOKEN'\\'', { replace: true })` to minimize exposure in browser history.'","description":"Reply to thread 12"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1189,"cache_read_input_tokens":34201,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1189},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"}},"requestId":"REDACTED","type":"assistant","uuid":"579c5c84-2eb5-4ba0-8240-949233de3857","timestamp":"2026-02-23T22:57:25.894Z"}

-0,0 +1,306 @@\\n+import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';\\n+import { useParams, useSearchParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';\\n+import { MatrixBackground } from EmailLoginForm } from '../components/auth/EmailLoginForm';\\n+import { WalletLoginButton } from '../components/auth/WalletLoginButton';\\n+import { DeviceWaitingScreen } from '../components/mfa/DeviceWaitingScreen';\\n+import { RecoveryInput } from '../components/mfa/RecoveryInput';\\n+import { checkInviteStatus, claimInvite } from '../services/invite.service';\\n+import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';\\n+import '../styles/invite-page.css';\\n+\\n+/** State machine for invite page */\\n+type InvitePageState = 'loading' | 'valid' | 'claiming' | 'claimed' | 'error';\\n+\\n+/** Error reason for display */\\n+type ErrorReason = 'expired' | 'claimed' | 'revoked' | 'invalid';\\n+\\n+type MfaView = 'waiting' | 'recovery';\\n+\\n+const ERROR_MESSAGES: Record<ErrorReason, string> = {\\n+  expired: 'this link has expired',\\n+  claimed: 'this link has already been claimed',\\n+  revoked: 'this link has been revoked',\\n+  invalid: 'invalid link',\\n+};\\n+\\n+/**\\n+ * Invite landing page for share link recipients.\\n+ *\\n+ * Standalone page (no AppShell) with:\\n+ * - Status check on mount via checkInviteStatus\\n+ * - Branded card with login CTA for valid invites\\n+ * - Inline auth (Google, Email, Wallet) matching Login.tsx\\n+ * - Auto-claim after authentication via useEffect watching isAuthenticated\\n+ * - Error cards with red border for expired/claimed/revoked/invalid\\n+ *\\n+ * The ephemeral private key is read from the hash fragment query param\\n+ * on initial render and stored in a ref (never in state, never logged).\\n+ */\\n+export function InvitePage() {\\n+  const { token } = useParams<{ token: string }>();\\n+  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();\\n+  const navigate = useNavigate();\\n+\\n+     99→    "body": "_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🔴 Critical_\n\n**Blocker: invite revocation is broken — `id`/token mismatch must be fixed before merge.**\n\nThe verification process correctly identifies this issue. The revoke flow will always fail with `400 Bad Request` because:\n\n1. `InviteResponseDto` (backend) does not include the invite's UUID `id` field.\n2. `fetchInvitesForItem` (frontend, `invite.service.ts` line 321) maps `inv.token` as `id`.\n3. `revokeInvite` passes this token string to `DELETE /shares/invites/:inviteId`.\n4. `ParseUUIDPipe` on the controller rejects base64url strings.\n\nApply Option 1 from the gaps summary — it is the correct fix:\n\n```diff\n// apps/api/src/shares/dto/invite-response.dto.ts\nexport class InviteResponseDto {\n+ `@ApiProperty`({ description: 'Invite UUID' })\n+ id: string;\n+\n  `@ApiProperty`({ description: 'Invite token (base64url)' })\n  token: string;\n  // ...rest of fields\n}\n```\n\n```diff\n// apps/web/src/services/invite.service.ts (around line 321)\n  return invites.map((inv) => ({\n-   id: inv.token,\n+   id: inv.id,\n    token: inv.token,\n    // ...\n  }));\n```\n\nAfter fixing the DTO, regenerate the Orval client so `InviteResponseDto.id` is available in the generated model.\n\n<details>\n<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn @.planning/phases/15-link-sharing/15-VERIFICATION.md around lines 7 - 20, Add\nthe missing UUID id to the backend DTO and thread it through the API and client:\nupdate InviteResponseDto to include the invite UUID field (id: string/UUID),\nupdate the backend list endpoint that returns InviteResponseDto so it populates\nthe real UUID (not token), remove any frontend mapping that sets inv.token as id\nin fetchInvitesForItem (invite.service.ts -> remove/migrate the mapping on line\nreferencing inv.token), ensure revokeInvite uses the real UUID field when\ncalling DELETE /shares/invites/:inviteId (controller currently uses\nParseUUIDPipe in share-invites.controller), then regenerate the Orval API client\nso the generated model includes InviteResponseDto.id and the frontend revoke\nflow passes a UUID to the DELETE call.\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:churro -->\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit -->"

'expired' }`, allowing an attacker to distinguish between a claimed invite, a revoked invite, and an expired one via HTTP 200 responses. The `ShareInvite` entity (line 56 of `share-invite.entity.ts`) confirms three distinct status values exist in the database.\n\nThe recommendation to return only `{ status: 'active' }` or throw `NotFoundException()` (404) for all non-active states has not been implemented. This creates the token-existence oracle flagged in H-01: an attacker who obtains a token (e.g., from browser history) can probe its status without the ephemeral key and learn whether the share was claimed or revoked.\n\n**Fix:** Update `getInviteStatus` to throw `NotFoundException()` for any non-`active` state:\n\n<details>\n<summary>Recommended change</summary>\n\n```typescript\nasync getInviteStatus(`@Param`('token') token: string): Promise<{ status: string }> {\n  const result = await this.sharesService.getInviteStatus(token);\n  if (!result || result.status !== 'active') {\n    throw new NotFoundException();\n  }\n  return { status: 'active' };\n}\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<details>\n<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn @.planning/security/REVIEW-phase15-link-sharing.md around lines 29 - 65, The\npublic getInviteStatus handler currently returns differentiated statuses (e.g.,\nresult?.status ?? 'expired'), leaking token existence and lifecycle info; update\nthe invites.controller.ts getInviteStatus(`@Param`('token') token) to call\nsharesService.getInviteStatus(token) and if the result is missing or\nresult.status !== 'active' throw NotFoundException(), otherwise return { status:\n'active' }; this collapses 'expired'/'claimed'/'revoked' into a generic 404 and\nprevents the token-existence oracle (see ShareInvite.status values in\nshare-invite.entity.ts).\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:churro -->\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit -->"

   117→    "body": "_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟡 Minor_\n\n**`itemId` missing `@IsUUID()` validation.**\n\n`@IsString()` + `@MinLength(1)` accepts any non-empty string. Since `itemId` represents a database UUID, malformed values will reach the DB query layer and produce unhelpful errors.
